
============================================================
COMPUTING METRICS
============================================================

--- RAG-IDB ---
give me the number of patients who have been diagnosed with tobacco use disorder.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DIAGNOSES."LONG_TITLE" = "Tobacco use disorder"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."long_title"', '=', 'tobacco use disorder']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DIAGNOSES."SHORT_TITLE" = "Tobacco use disorder"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."short_title"', '=', 'tobacco use disorder']]}
----------

provide me the number of patients who have been diagnosed with chordae tendinae rupture.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT SUBJECT_ID) FROM DIAGNOSES WHERE LONG_TITLE = 'Chordae tendinae rupture'
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': [], 'tab': ['diagnoses'], 'cond': [['long_title', '=', 'chordae tendinae rupture']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DIAGNOSES."SHORT_TITLE" = "Chordae tendinae rupture"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."short_title"', '=', 'chordae tendinae rupture']]}
----------

Calulate the total number of patients who had icd9 code 5771
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DIAGNOSES."ICD9_CODE" = "5771"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."icd9_code"', '=', '5771']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN PROCEDURES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PROCEDURES.HADM_ID WHERE PROCEDURES."ICD9_CODE" = "5771"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'procedures'], 'cond': [['procedures."icd9_code"', '=', '5771']]}
----------

provide the number of patients whose procedure icd9 code is 8848.

{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'procedures'], 'cond': [['procedures."icd9_code"', '=', '8848']]}

How many married patients died in or before 2165?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."MARITAL_STATUS" = "MARRIED" AND DEMOGRAPHIC."EXPIRE_FLAG" = "1" AND DEMOGRAPHIC."DOD_YEAR" <= "2165"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic'], 'cond': [['demographic."dod_year"', '<=', '2165'], ['demographic."expire_flag"', '=', '1'], ['demographic."marital_status"', '=', 'married']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."MARITAL_STATUS" = "MARRIED" AND DEMOGRAPHIC."DOD_YEAR" <= "2165.0"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic'], 'cond': [['demographic."dod_year"', '<=', '2165.0'], ['demographic."marital_status"', '=', 'married']]}
----------

Look for the number of elective hospital admission patients who had a unilateral radical neck dissection procedure.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN PROCEDURES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PROCEDURES.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."ADMISSION_TYPE" = "ELECTIVE" AND PROCEDURES."LONG_TITLE" = "Unilateral radical neck dissection"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'procedures'], 'cond': [['demographic."admission_type"', '=', 'elective'], ['procedures."long_title"', '=', 'unilateral radical neck dissection']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN PROCEDURES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PROCEDURES.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."ADMISSION_TYPE" = "ELECTIVE" AND PROCEDURES."SHORT_TITLE" = "Unilat rad neck dissect"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'procedures'], 'cond': [['demographic."admission_type"', '=', 'elective'], ['procedures."short_title"', '=', 'unilat rad neck dissect']]}

Calculate the number of patients admitted to emergency who had an ld body fluid test by lab
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN LAB on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = LAB.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."ADMISSION_TYPE" = "EMERGENCY" AND LAB."FLUID" = "LD Body Fluid"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'lab'], 'cond': [['demographic."admission_type"', '=', 'emergency'], ['lab."fluid"', '=', 'ld body fluid']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN LAB on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = LAB.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."ADMISSION_TYPE" = "EMERGENCY" AND LAB."LABEL" = "LD, Body Fluid"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'lab'], 'cond': [['demographic."admission_type"', '=', 'emergency'], ['lab."label"', '=', 'ld, body fluid']]}
----------

provide the number of patients whose death status is 0 and primary disease is aortic insufficiency/re-do sternotomy; aortic valve replacement.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC.SUBJECT_ID) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN PROCEDURES ON DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PROCEDURES.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC.EXPIRE_FLAG = 0 AND PROCEDURES.LONG_TITLE = 'aortic insufficiency/re-do sternotomy; aortic valve replacement'
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': [], 'tab': ['demographic', 'procedures'], 'cond': [['demographic.expire_flag', '=', '0'], ['procedures.long_title', '=', 'aortic insufficiency/re-do sternotomy; aortic valve replacement']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."EXPIRE_FLAG" = "0" AND DEMOGRAPHIC."DIAGNOSIS" = "AORTIC INSUFFICIENCY\RE-DO STERNOTOMY; AORTIC VALVE REPLACEMENT"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic'], 'cond': [['demographic."diagnosis"', '=', 'aortic insufficiency\\re-do sternotomy; aortic valve replacement'], ['demographic."expire_flag"', '=', '0']]}
----------

provide the number of patients with a diagnoses of personal history of other malignant neoplasm of rectum, rectosigmoid junction and anus and are still alive.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DIAGNOSES."LONG_TITLE" = "Personal history of other malignant neoplasm of rectum, rectosigmoid junction and anus" AND DEMOGRAPHIC."EXPIRE_FLAG" = 0

how many patients are married and diagnosed with primary disease coronary artery disease or coronary artery bypass graft with mvr maze?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."MARITAL_STATUS" = "MARRIED" AND (DIAGNOSES."LONG_TITLE" = "Coronary artery disease" OR DIAGNOSES."LONG_TITLE" = "Coronary artery bypass graft with MVR maze")

{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['prescriptions."route"', '=', 'iv']]}

GOLD: SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."AGE" < "31" AND DIAGNOSES."ICD9_CODE" = "V140"
PRED_OUT: Counter({(0,): 1})
GOLD_OUT: Counter({(2,): 1})


what is the number of patients with acute vascular insufficiency of intestine who are less than 50 years of age?

FROM DEMOGRAPHIC
